L2210: Table 70: Summary of Bioanalytical Method Performance of (b) (4) 1100004
L2211: Bioanalytical method review summary	
Determination of DCC-3014 and DP-7005 in Human K2-EDTA Plasma by LC-MS/MS ((b) (4) 1100004). This method underwent three amendments.
Validation amendment report No.1: Partial Validation ((b) (4) 1100004 am1). This amendment extended the calibration standards for DP-7005 only.
Validation amendment report No.2: Additional Long-Term Stability, Cross Validation, and Interference Check ((b) (4) 1100004 am2). This amendment was created to report long-term stability in human K2-EDTA plasma for DCC-3014 stored for 372 days at -70°C and stored for 525 days at -20°C. Additionally, the assay procedure underwent cross validation for laboratory relocation, meeting the established acceptance criteria. This amendment also reported the interference evaluation of the samples, for each analyte at the concentration of the LLOQ calibration standard, in the presence of itraconazole and hydroxyitraconazole, each at about 3000 ng/mL, and rabeprazole at about 1000 ng/mL.
Validation amendment report No.3: Additional Matrix Effect ((b) (4) 1100004 am3). For this post-validation study, additional analyses were conducted under (b) (4) 1100004 using (b) (4) -2638 to evaluate matrix effect using the procedure and criteria in the M10 Guidance.

Method description	
The method was validated for a range of 1-2000 ng/mL for DCC-3014 based on the analysis of 50 µL of human K2-EDTA plasma. Human plasma containing DCC-3014 and the internal standard, DCC-3014-13CD3, was extracted using protein precipitation and analyzed by a Sciex API 5000 LC-MS/MS equipped with an HPLC column. The peak area of the m/z 432.1→306.1 DCC-3014 product ion was measured against the peak area of the m/z 436.1→310.1 DCC-3014-13CD3 internal standard product ion. The peak area of the m/z 390.1→306.2. Quantitation was performed using separate weighted 1/x2 (for each analyte) linear least squares regression analyses generated from calibration standards prepared on the day of extraction.

Materials used for calibration curve & concentration	
Stock solutions of reference standards and internal standards: dimethyl sulfoxide/methanol (1:1). The solvent used to prepare intermediate solutions of reference standards from stock solutions: methanol/water (1:1). Combined working internal standard solution: methanol/water (1:1) Matrix of standard calibration curve: human K2-EDTA plasma. DCC-3014: 1, 2, 10, 50, 250, 1000, 1800, and 2000 ng/mL

Validated assay range	
DCC-3014: 1-2000 ng/mL

Material used for QCs & concentration	
Matrix of QCs: human K2-EDTA plasma
DCC-3014: 1 ng/mL (LLOQ), 3 ng/mL (QC low), 1000 ng/mL (QC medium), 1600 ng/mL (QC high), 10,000 ng/mL (QC very high)

Minimum required dilutions (MRDs)	
N/A

Source & lot of reagents (LBA)	
This method did not use any critical reagents.

Regression model & weighting	
Separate weighted 1/x2 (for each analyte) linear least squares regression

Validation parameters

L2227: Figure 3 displays three bar charts showing the steady-state inhibition of CSF1-stimulated cFOS expression by Vimseltinib (DCC-3014) after six days of oral dosing. The Y-axis represents the percentage of Vehicle Control (\pm CSF1), and the X-axis represents Time (hr) (0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 12, 18, 24, 48). The Vehicle + CSF1 condition is shown as a control bar (approx. 100% of Vehicle Control).
The three charts correspond to different doses of DCC-3014:
DCC-3014 (10 mg/kg QDx6): Shows strong inhibition of cFOS expression over time, reaching approximately 25% of Vehicle Control at 48 hours.
DCC-3014 (3 mg/kg QDx6): Shows moderate inhibition of cFOS expression over time, reaching approximately 40% of Vehicle Control at 48 hours.
DCC-3014 (1 mg/kg QDx6): Shows minimal inhibition of cFOS expression over time, remaining close to the Vehicle + CSF1 control level (around 80-90% of Vehicle Control).
The data demonstrates a dose-dependent inhibition of CSF1-stimulated cFOS expression by DCC-3014.

Mean Group Score
0: Normal Bone
1: Minimal lesions- some roughening of the isosurface. small areas of apparent bone resorption
2: Mild- more numerous lesions, significant roughening of the isosurface with full thickness lesions apparent
3: Moderate- full thickness lesions that are larger and more numerous
4: Marked- many, large, full thickness lesions with significant distortion of remaining structure and marked bone loss.

L2232: Figure 8 is a timeline diagram illustrating the Motion Study Schema for the Phase 2 trial of vimseltinib (ROMVIMZA) in tenosynovial giant cell tumors (TGCT). The study is divided into two main parts: Part 1 (Double-blind) and Part 2 (Open-label), followed by an Extension of Treatment phase, leading to End of Treatment and Disease Follow-up.
Timeline Details:
Randomization: 2:1 ratio (Vimseltinib:Placebo).
Enrollment Target: N=120.
Part 1 Double-blind (24 Weeks): Vimseltinib 30 mg biw (n=80) and Placebo biw (n=40).
Part 2 Open-label: Vimseltinib 30 mg biw (all participants).
Exploratory Endpoints: Occur during the Extension of Treatment phase.
End of Treatment: Marks the end of active treatment.
Disease Follow-upa: Follows End of Treatment, lasting up to 2 years.
Assessments: MRI, ROM, and PRO assessments are scheduled every 3 months throughout the study duration.

L2236: Figure 12 displays four line graphs (A, B, C, D) showing the change in various patient-reported outcomes (PROs) from Baseline to Week 25 for patients treated with Vimseltinib (N=83) compared to Placebo (N=40).
A. Change in Physical Function (PROMIS-PF) from Baseline to Week 25
The Y-axis represents the LS Mean (SE) change in PROMIS-PF score, ranging from -2 to 8. The X-axis represents Weeks (0, 5, 9, 13, 17, 21, 25). A dashed line indicates the MCID of 3.
The Vimseltinib group shows a significant improvement, increasing from approximately 0 at Baseline to approximately 4.5 at Week 25. The Placebo group shows minimal change, remaining around 1.5. The difference between the groups is statistically significant (p\text{-value}=0.0007).
B. Change in Worst Stiffness NRS from Baseline to Week 25
The Y-axis represents the LS Mean (SE) change in Worst Stiffness NRS score, ranging from -3 to 1. The X-axis represents Weeks (0, 5, 9, 13, 17, 21, 25). A dashed line indicates the MCID of -2.
The Vimseltinib group shows a significant improvement, decreasing from approximately 0 at Baseline to approximately -2.5 at Week 25. The Placebo group shows minimal change, remaining around 0. The difference between the groups is statistically significant (p\text{-value}<0.0001).
C. Change in EQ-VAS from Baseline to Week 25
The Y-axis represents the LS Mean (SE) change in EQ-VAS score, ranging from -5 to 20. The X-axis represents Weeks (0, 5, 9, 13, 17, 21, 25). A dashed line indicates the MCID of 7.
The Vimseltinib group shows a significant improvement, increasing from approximately 0 at Baseline to approximately 14 at Week 25. The Placebo group shows minimal change, remaining around 5. The difference between the groups is statistically significant (p\text{-value}=0.0155).
D. Percent Change from Baseline in Worst Pain NRS to Week 25
The Y-axis represents the Mean \pm SE percent change in Worst Pain NRS score, ranging from -50 to 50. The X-axis represents Weeks (0, 5, 9, 13, 17, 21, 25).
The Vimseltinib group shows a significant improvement, decreasing from approximately 0 at Baseline to approximately -40 at Week 25. The Placebo group shows minimal change, remaining around -10. The difference between the groups is statistically significant (p\text{-value}<0.0001).

L2246: Flowchart illustrating the Schematic Illustration of Dose Escalation, Phase 1/2 Study.
The study is divided into two main groups: MST (Multi-Synovial Tumor) and TGCT (Tenosynovial Giant Cell Tumor).
MST Pathway:
Cohort 1 (N = 3-6): No loading dose, 10 mg QD.
Cohort 2 (N = 3-6): 10 mg x 5 loading dose, 10 mg x 2/week maintenance.
Cohort 3 (N = 3-6): 20 mg x 5 loading dose, 20 mg x 1/week Maintenance.
Cohort 4 (N = 3-6): 20 mg x 5 loading dose, 20 mg x 2/week maintenance.
Cohort 5 (N = 3-6): 30 mg x 5 loading dose, 30 mg x 2/week maintenance.
Cohort 6 (N = 3-6): 40 mg x 5 loading dose, 40 mg x 2/week maintenance.
Cohort 7 (N = 3-6): 50 mg x 3 loading dose, 20 mg QD maintenance.
TGCT Pathway:
Cohort 5 (N = 3-6): 30 mg x 5 loading dose, 30 mg x 2/week maintenance.
Cohort 8 (N = 3-6): 30 mg x 3 loading dose, 10 mg QD maintenance.
Cohort 9 (N = 3-6): 20 mg x 3 loading dose, 6 mg QD maintenance.
Both MST and TGCT pathways lead to Additional cohorts (N = 3-6), where Loading/maintenance or QD dosing is determined by review of safety, efficacy, PK, and PD, which then leads to MTD or RP2D determination.

L2256: Figure 33 displays two scatter plots showing the effects of vimseltinib on dabigatran exposure following staggered dosing. The x-axis for both plots represents "Hours after Vimseltinib Dosing (hrs)" (0 to 4 hours). The y-axis represents the "Ratios of Dabigatran".
The left plot, titled "AUC Ratios of Dabigatran", shows the Area Under the Curve (AUC) ratios. The right plot, titled "C_{max} Ratios of Dabigatran", shows the maximum concentration (C_{max}) ratios. Both plots compare dabigatran exposure with and without co-administration of vimseltinib, using four different IC_{50} values for P-gp inhibition: 50 (Triangle), 100 (Square), 200 (Circle), and 1000 (X).
In both plots, the ratios generally decrease as the time after vimseltinib dosing increases, indicating reduced dabigatran exposure. The ratios are highest at 0 hours (immediately after vimseltinib dosing) and lowest at 4 hours. The 1000-fold lower IC_{50} (X) consistently shows the highest ratios, while the 50-fold lower IC_{50} (Triangle) shows the lowest ratios.
